Model before rewriting:

find x: int(5..7)
find y: int(0..3)
find z: int(0..4)

such that

!(x % y % z = 3)

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#order_1: bool
  find x#order_2: bool
  find x#order_3: bool
new constraints:
  x#order_1
  or([!(x#order_2),x#order_1;int(1..)])
  or([!(x#order_3),x#order_2;int(1..)])
  or([and([SATInt(Order, [x#order_1,x#order_2,x#order_3;int(1..)] [5, 7]) >= 5,SATInt(Order, [x#order_1,x#order_2,x#order_3;int(1..)] [5, 7]) <= 7;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

SATInt(Order, [x#order_1,x#order_2,x#order_3;int(1..)] [5, 7]) >= 5
   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
__0
new variables:
  findAux __0: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__0 <-> integer-weighted(1*x#order_2, 1*x#order_3) GreaterEqual 0) using None with [PbTermGroup { start: 0, end: 2, structure: Chain }]

--

SATInt(Order, [x#order_1,x#order_2,x#order_3;int(1..)] [5, 7]) <= 7
   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
__1
new variables:
  findAux __1: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__1 <-> integer-weighted(1*x#order_2, 1*x#order_3) LessEqual 2) using None with [PbTermGroup { start: 0, end: 2, structure: Chain }]

--

y
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
y
new variables:
  find y#order_1: bool
  find y#order_2: bool
  find y#order_3: bool
  find y#order_4: bool
new constraints:
  y#order_1
  or([!(y#order_2),y#order_1;int(1..)])
  or([!(y#order_3),y#order_2;int(1..)])
  or([!(y#order_4),y#order_3;int(1..)])
  or([and([SATInt(Order, [y#order_1,y#order_2,y#order_3,y#order_4;int(1..)] [0, 3]) >= 0,SATInt(Order, [y#order_1,y#order_2,y#order_3,y#order_4;int(1..)] [0, 3]) <= 3;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

SATInt(Order, [y#order_1,y#order_2,y#order_3,y#order_4;int(1..)] [0, 3]) >= 0
   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
__2
new variables:
  findAux __2: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__2 <-> integer-weighted(1*y#order_2, 1*y#order_3, 1*y#order_4) GreaterEqual 0) using None with [PbTermGroup { start: 0, end: 3, structure: Chain }]

--

SATInt(Order, [y#order_1,y#order_2,y#order_3,y#order_4;int(1..)] [0, 3]) <= 3
   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
__3
new variables:
  findAux __3: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__3 <-> integer-weighted(1*y#order_2, 1*y#order_3, 1*y#order_4) LessEqual 3) using None with [PbTermGroup { start: 0, end: 3, structure: Chain }]

--

z
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
z
new variables:
  find z#order_1: bool
  find z#order_2: bool
  find z#order_3: bool
  find z#order_4: bool
  find z#order_5: bool
new constraints:
  z#order_1
  or([!(z#order_2),z#order_1;int(1..)])
  or([!(z#order_3),z#order_2;int(1..)])
  or([!(z#order_4),z#order_3;int(1..)])
  or([!(z#order_5),z#order_4;int(1..)])
  or([and([SATInt(Order, [z#order_1,z#order_2,z#order_3,z#order_4,z#order_5;int(1..)] [0, 4]) >= 0,SATInt(Order, [z#order_1,z#order_2,z#order_3,z#order_4,z#order_5;int(1..)] [0, 4]) <= 4;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

SATInt(Order, [z#order_1,z#order_2,z#order_3,z#order_4,z#order_5;int(1..)] [0, 4]) >= 0
   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
__4
new variables:
  findAux __4: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__4 <-> integer-weighted(1*z#order_2, 1*z#order_3, 1*z#order_4, 1*z#order_5) GreaterEqual 0) using None with [PbTermGroup { start: 0, end: 4, structure: Chain }]

--

SATInt(Order, [z#order_1,z#order_2,z#order_3,z#order_4,z#order_5;int(1..)] [0, 4]) <= 4
   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
__5
new variables:
  findAux __5: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__5 <-> integer-weighted(1*z#order_2, 1*z#order_3, 1*z#order_4, 1*z#order_5) LessEqual 4) using None with [PbTermGroup { start: 0, end: 4, structure: Chain }]

--

x
   ~~> integer_decision_representation_order ([("SAT", 9500)])
SATInt(Order, [x#order_1,x#order_2,x#order_3;int(1..)] [5, 7])

--

y
   ~~> integer_decision_representation_order ([("SAT", 9500)])

--

z
   ~~> integer_decision_representation_order ([("SAT", 9500)])

--

!(x#order_3)
   ~~> apply_tseytin_not ([("SAT", 9005)])
